Commit: https://github.com/plone/Products.CMFPlone/commit/d320469ff84c94814bb8a99d3cce27ec9ac05fb8

Do not use pkg_resources.require.

It fails on Python 3.13 when the package is an editable install and you are using pip.
Buildout has no problems with it. :-)
